Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1444-1463
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1444 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1445 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1446 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1447 |     }
1448 | 
1449 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1450 |     Node n, n2;
1451 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1452 |          res == null && n != null;
1453 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1454 |       if (node2 == null) {
1455 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1456 |       }
1457 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1458 |       if (res != null) {
1459 |         return res;
1460 |       }
1461 |     }
1462 |     return res;
1463 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getPrototype()

/**
   * Gets the {@code prototype} property of this function type. This is
   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.
   */
Method code:
308 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {
309 |     // lazy initialization of the prototype field
310 |     if (prototypeSlot == null) {
311 |       String refName = getReferenceName();
312 |       if (refName == null) {
313 |         // Someone is trying to access the prototype of a structural function.
314 |         // We don't want to give real properties to this prototype, because
315 |         // then it would propagate to all structural functions.
316 |         setPrototype(

318 |            null);
319 |       } else {
320 |         setPrototype(
321 |             new PrototypeObjectType(
322 |                 registry,
323 |                 this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype",
324 |                 registry.getNativeObjectType(OBJECT_TYPE),
325 |                 isNativeObjectType()),
326 |             null);
327 |       }
328 |     }
329 |     return (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
330 |   }

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.collectDefines(Node,GlobalNamespace)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 172-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds all defines, and creates a {@link DefineInfo} data structure for
   * each one.
   * @return A map of {@link DefineInfo} structures, keyed by name.
   */
Method code:
172 |   private Map<String, DefineInfo> collectDefines(Node root,
173 |       GlobalNamespace namespace) {
174 |     // Find all the global names with a @define annotation
175 |     List<Name> allDefines = Lists.newArrayList();
176 |     for (Name name : namespace.getNameIndex().values()) {
177 |       Ref decl = name.getDeclaration();
178 |       if (name.docInfo != null && name.docInfo.isDefine()) {
179 |         // Process defines should not depend on check types being enabled,
180 |         // so we look for the JSDoc instead of the inferred type.
181 |         if (isValidDefineType(name.docInfo.getType())) {
182 |           allDefines.add(name);
183 |         } else {
184 |           JSError error = JSError.make(
185 |               decl.getSourceName(),
186 |               decl.node, INVALID_DEFINE_TYPE_ERROR);
187 |           compiler.report(error);
188 |         }
189 |       } else {
190 |         for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
191 |           if (ref == decl) {
192 |             // Declarations were handled above.
193 |             continue;
194 |           }
195 | 
196 |           Node n = ref.node;
197 |           Node parent = ref.node.getParent();
198 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
199 |           if (info == null &&
200 |               parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
201 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
202 |           }
203 | 
204 |           if (info != null && info.isDefine()) {
205 |             allDefines.add(name);
206 |             break;
207 |           }
208 |         }
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     CollectDefines pass = new CollectDefines(compiler, allDefines);
213 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, pass);
214 |     return pass.getAllDefines();
215 |   }

===== M0030 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1508-1555
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1508 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1509 |     if (type != node.getType()
1510 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1511 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1512 |       return false;
1513 |     }
1514 | 
1515 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1516 |       return false;
1517 |     }
1518 | 
1519 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1520 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1521 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1522 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1523 |         return false;
1524 |       }
1525 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1526 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1527 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1528 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1529 |         return false;
1530 |       }
1531 | 
1532 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1533 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1534 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1535 |         return false;
1536 |       }
1537 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1538 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1539 |         return false;
1540 |       }
1541 |     }
1542 | 
1543 |     if (recurse) {
1544 |       Node n, n2;
1545 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1546 |            n != null;
1547 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1548 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1549 |           return false;
1550 |         }
1551 |       }
1552 |     }
1553 | 
1554 |     return true;
1555 |   }

===== M0035 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 237-268
Documentation:

Method code:
237 |     @Override
238 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
239 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
240 |       if (n.isName()) {
241 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
242 |           return;
243 |         }
244 | 
245 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
246 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top level variable.
247 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
248 |         if (var != null) {
249 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
250 |         }
251 | 
252 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
253 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
254 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
255 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
256 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
257 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
258 |           if (assertOnChange) {
259 |             String name = n.getString();
260 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
261 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
262 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
263 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
264 |           }
265 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
266 |         }
267 |       }
268 |     }

790 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
791 |     boolean valid = false;
792 |     if (i == 1) {

799 |

803 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
804 |     }

===== M0060 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 784-927
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
784 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
785 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
786 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
787 |     // that we know to be safe
788 |     switch (n.getType()) {
789 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
790 |       case Token.AND:
791 |       case Token.BLOCK:
792 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
793 |       case Token.HOOK:
794 |       case Token.IF:
795 |       case Token.IN:
796 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
797 |       case Token.NUMBER:
798 |       case Token.OR:
799 |       case Token.THIS:
800 |       case Token.TRUE:
801 |       case Token.FALSE:
802 |       case Token.NULL:
803 |       case Token.STRING:
804 |       case Token.SWITCH:
805 |       case Token.TRY:
806 |       case Token.EMPTY:
807 |         break;
808 | 
809 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
810 |       case Token.THROW:
811 |         return true;
812 | 
813 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
814 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
815 |           return true;
816 |         }
817 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
818 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
819 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
820 |             return true;
821 |           }
822 |         }
823 |         return false;
824 | 
825 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
826 |       case Token.REGEXP:
827 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
828 |           return true;
829 |         }
830 |         break;
831 | 
832 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
833 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
834 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
835 |           return true;
836 |         }
837 |         break;
838 | 
839 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
840 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
841 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
842 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
843 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
844 | 
845 |       case Token.NEW:
846 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
847 |           return true;
848 |         }
849 | 
850 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
851 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
852 |           // side-effects
853 |           break;
854 |         }
855 |         return true;
856 | 
857 |       case Token.CALL:
858 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
859 |         // side effect property set.
860 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
861 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
862 |           // side-effects
863 |           break;
864 |         }
865 |         return true;
866 | 
867 |       default:
868 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
869 |           break;
870 |         }
871 | 
872 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
873 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
874 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
875 |             return true;
876 |           }
877 | 
878 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
879 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
880 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
881 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
882 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
883 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
884 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
885 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
886 |             return true;
887 |           }
888 | 
889 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
890 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
891 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
892 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
893 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
894 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
895 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
896 |               return false;
897 |             }
898 | 
899 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
900 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
901 |             // other objects.
902 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
903 |             // side-effect.
904 |             while (isGet(current)) {
905 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
906 |             }
907 | 
908 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
909 |           } else {
910 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
911 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
912 |             // preserve existing behavior.
913 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
914 |           }
915 |         }
916 | 
917 |         return true;
918 |     }
919 | 
920 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
921 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
922 |         return true;
923 |       }
924 |     }
925 | 
926 |     return false;
927 |   }

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

===== M0066 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 640-672
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
640 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
641 |       Preconditions.checkState(
642 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
643 |       Node previous = null;
644 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
645 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
646 |       // are already in the right place.
647 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
648 |         previous = current;
649 |         current = current.getNext();
650 |       }
651 | 
652 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
653 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
654 |       while (current != null) {
655 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
656 |         Node next = current.getNext();
657 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
658 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
659 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
660 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
661 | 
662 |           // Readd the function at the top of the function body (after any
663 |           // previous declarations).
664 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
665 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
666 |         } else {
667 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
668 |           previous = current;
669 |         }
670 |         current = next;
671 |       }
672 |     }

===== M0075 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1752-1787
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1752 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1753 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1754 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1755 |     }
1756 | 
1757 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1758 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1759 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1760 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1761 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1762 |       skipEOLs();
1763 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1764 |         next();
1765 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1766 |       }
1767 |     }
1768 | 
1769 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1770 | 
1771 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1772 |       next();
1773 |       skipEOLs();
1774 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1775 |       if (memberType != null) {
1776 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1777 | 
1778 |         skipEOLs();
1779 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1780 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1781 |         }
1782 | 
1783 |         next();
1784 |       }
1785 |     }
1786 |     return typeNameNode;
1787 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefaultPassConfig.anonymous_L763.createInternal(AbstractCompiler)

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */

201 |       }

279 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
280 |                   }

807 |                   } else {

809 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",

817 |                 case DEFINE:
818 |                 case RETURN:

820 |                 case TYPE:
821 |                 case TYPEDEF:
822 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
823 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
824 | 
825 |                   Node typeNode = null;
826 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
827 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
828 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
829 |                     // it as the unknown type.
830 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);

838 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
839 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
840 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
841 |                     }

850 |                       case DEFINE:
851 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
852 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
853 |                               lineno, charno);
854 |                         }
855 |                         break;
856 | 
857 |                       case RETURN:
858 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
859 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
860 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
861 |                           break;
862 |                         }
863 | 
864 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
865 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
866 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
867 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
868 | 
869 |                           String returnDescription =
870 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
871 | 
872 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
873 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
874 |                                 returnDescription);
875 |                           }
876 | 
877 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
878 |                         } else {
879 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
880 |                         }
881 |                         continue retry;
882 | 
883 |                       case THIS:
884 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
885 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
886 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
887 |                         }
888 |                         break;
889 | 
890 |                       case TYPE:
891 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
892 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
893 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
894 |                         }
895 |                         break;
896 | 
897 |                       case TYPEDEF:
898 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
899 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
900 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
901 |                         }
902 |                         break;
903 |                     }
904 |                   }
905 | 
906 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
907 |                   continue retry;
908 |               }
909 |             }
910 |           }
911 |           break;
912 | 
913 |         case EOC:
914 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
915 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
916 |           }
917 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
918 |           return true;
919 | 
920 |         case EOF:
921 |           // discard any accumulated information
922 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
923 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
924 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
925 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
926 |           return false;
927 | 
928 |         case EOL:
929 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
930 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
931 |           }
932 |           token = next();
933 |           continue retry;
934 | 
935 |         default:
936 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
937 |             token = next();
938 |             continue retry;
939 |           } else {
940 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
941 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
942 |             continue retry;
943 |           }
944 |       }
945 | 
946 |       // next token
947 |       token = next();
948 |     }
949 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

167 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
168 |       if (NodeUtil.isExprAssign(n)) {
169 |         Node assign = n.getFirstChild();
170 |         Node lhs = assign.getFirstChild();
171 |         if (lhs.isGetProp() && isMarkedExpose(assign)) {
172 |           exposedProperties.add(lhs.getLastChild().getString());
173 |         }

175 |           n.isString() &&
176 |           isMarkedExpose(n)) {
177 |         exposedProperties.add(n.getString());
178 |       }
179 |     }

===== M0130 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

===== M0131 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0141 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.anonymous_L1631.call()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1632-1688
Documentation:

Method code:
1632 |       @Override
1633 |       public Void call() throws Exception {
1634 |         if (options.printInputDelimiter) {
1635 |           if ((cb.getLength() > 0) && !cb.endsWith("\n")) {
1636 |             cb.append("\n");  // Make sure that the label starts on a new line
1637 |           }
1638 |           Preconditions.checkState(root.isScript());
1639 | 
1640 |           String delimiter = options.inputDelimiter;
1641 | 
1642 |           String inputName = root.getInputId().getIdName();
1643 |           String sourceName = root.getSourceFileName();
1644 |           Preconditions.checkState(sourceName != null);
1645 |           Preconditions.checkState(!sourceName.isEmpty());
1646 | 
1647 |           delimiter = delimiter
1648 |               .replaceAll("%name%", Matcher.quoteReplacement(inputName))
1649 |               .replaceAll("%num%", String.valueOf(inputSeqNum));
1650 | 
1651 |           cb.append(delimiter)
1652 |             .append("\n");
1653 |         }
1654 |         if (root.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
1655 |             root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null) {
1656 |           cb.append("/*\n")
1657 |             .append(root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense())
1658 |             .append("*/\n");
1659 |         }
1660 | 
1661 |         // If there is a valid source map, then indicate to it that the current
1662 |         // root node's mappings are offset by the given string builder buffer.
1663 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
1664 |           sourceMap.setStartingPosition(
1665 |               cb.getLineIndex(), cb.getColumnIndex());
1666 |         }
1667 | 
1668 |         // if LanguageMode is ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT, only print 'use strict'
1669 |         // for the first input file
1670 |         String code = toSource(root, sourceMap, inputSeqNum == 0);
1671 |         if (!code.isEmpty()) {
1672 |           cb.append(code);
1673 | 
1674 |           // In order to avoid parse ambiguity when files are concatenated
1675 |           // together, all files should end in a semi-colon. Do a quick
1676 |           // heuristic check if there's an obvious semi-colon already there.
1677 |           int length = code.length();
1678 |           char lastChar = code.charAt(length - 1);
1679 |           char secondLastChar = length >= 2 ?
1680 |               code.charAt(length - 2) : '\0';
1681 |           boolean hasSemiColon = lastChar == ';' ||
1682 |               (lastChar == '\n' && secondLastChar == ';');
1683 |           if (!hasSemiColon) {
1684 |             cb.append(";");
1685 |           }
1686 |         }
1687 |         return null;
1688 |       }

774 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
775 |     boolean valid = false;
776 |     if (i == 0) {
777 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
778 |     } else if (i == 1) {
779 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
780 |     } else {
781 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
782 |     }
783 |     if (!valid) {

786 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
787 |     }

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;
797 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
798 |       return item.getNext();
799 |     } else {
800 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
801 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
802 |         return item.chain(result);
803 |       } else {
804 |         return item;
805 |       }
806 |     }
807 |   }

   * Converts the main parse tree back to js code.
   */
Method code:
1514 |   public String toSource() {
1515 |     return runInCompilerThread(new Callable<String>() {
1516 |       @Override
1517 |       public String call() throws Exception {
1518 |         Tracer tracer = newTracer("toSource");
1519 |         try {
1520 |           CodeBuilder cb = new CodeBuilder();
1521 |           if (jsRoot != null) {
1522 |             int i = 0;
1523 |             for (Node scriptNode = jsRoot.getFirstChild();
1524 |                  scriptNode != null;
1525 |                  scriptNode = scriptNode.getNext()) {
1526 |               toSource(cb, i++, scriptNode);
1527 |             }
1528 |           }
1529 |           return cb.toString();
1530 |         } finally {
1531 |           stopTracer(tracer, "toSource");
1532 |         }
1533 |       }
1534 |     });
1535 |   }

   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.

Method code:
2812 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2813 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
2814 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2815 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2816 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2817 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2818 |       if (parent.isAssign()) {
2819 |         // on ASSIGNs
2820 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2821 |       } else if (parent.isName()) {
2822 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2823 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2824 |       }
2825 |     }
2826 |     return fnInfo;

===== M0194 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java
Original range: 83-166
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   @Override
84 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
85 |     // VOID nodes appear when there are extra semicolons at the BLOCK level.
86 |     // I've been unable to think of any cases where this indicates a bug,
87 |     // and apparently some people like keeping these semicolons around,
88 |     // so we'll allow it.
89 |     if (n.isEmpty() ||
90 |         n.isComma()) {
91 |       return;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     if (parent == null) {
95 |       return;
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     int pt = parent.getType();
99 |     if (pt == Token.COMMA) {
100 |       Node gramps = parent.getParent();
101 |       if (gramps.isCall() &&
102 |           parent == gramps.getFirstChild()) {
103 |         // Semantically, a direct call to eval is different from an indirect
104 |         // call to an eval. See Ecma-262 S15.1.2.1. So it's ok for the first
105 |         // expression to a comma to be a no-op if it's used to indirect
106 |         // an eval.
107 |         if (n == parent.getFirstChild() &&
108 |             parent.getChildCount() == 2 &&
109 |             n.getNext().isName() &&
110 |             "eval".equals(n.getNext().getString())) {
111 |           return;
112 |         }
113 |       }
114 | 
115 |       if (n == parent.getLastChild()) {
116 |         for (Node an : parent.getAncestors()) {
117 |           int ancestorType = an.getType();
118 |           if (ancestorType == Token.COMMA)
119 |             continue;
120 |           if (ancestorType != Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
121 |               ancestorType != Token.BLOCK)
122 |             return;
123 |           else
124 |             break;
125 |         }
126 |       }
127 |     } else if (pt != Token.EXPR_RESULT && pt != Token.BLOCK) {
128 |       if (pt == Token.FOR && parent.getChildCount() == 4 &&
129 |           (n == parent.getFirstChild() ||
130 |            n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext())) {
131 |         // Fall through and look for warnings for the 1st and 3rd child
132 |         // of a for.
133 |       } else {
134 |         return;  // it might be ok to not have a side-effect
135 |       }
136 |     }
137 | 
138 |     boolean isSimpleOp = NodeUtil.isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType());
139 |     if (isSimpleOp ||
140 |         !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(n, t.getCompiler())) {
141 |       if (n.isQualifiedName() && n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
142 |         // This no-op statement was there so that JSDoc information could
143 |         // be attached to the name. This check should not complain about it.
144 |         return;
145 |       } else if (n.isExprResult()) {
146 |         // we already reported the problem when we visited the child.
147 |         return;
148 |       }
149 | 
150 |       String msg = "This code lacks side-effects. Is there a bug?";
151 |       if (n.isString()) {
152 |         msg = "Is there a missing '+' on the previous line?";
153 |       } else if (isSimpleOp) {
154 |         msg = "The result of the '" + Token.name(n.getType()).toLowerCase() +
155 |             "' operator is not being used.";
156 |       }
157 | 
158 |       t.getCompiler().report(
159 |           t.makeError(n, level, USELESS_CODE_ERROR, msg));
160 |       // TODO(johnlenz): determine if it is necessary to
161 |       // try to protect side-effect free statements as well.
162 |       if (!NodeUtil.isStatement(n)) {
163 |         problemNodes.add(n);
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |   }

===== M0196 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1902-1904
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1902 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1903 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1904 |   }

===== M0197 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isControlStructure(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1617-1634
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a FOR, DO, WHILE, WITH, or IF node.
   */
Method code:
1617 |   static boolean isControlStructure(Node n) {
1618 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1619 |       case Token.FOR:
1620 |       case Token.DO:
1621 |       case Token.WHILE:
1622 |       case Token.WITH:
1623 |       case Token.IF:
1624 |       case Token.LABEL:
1625 |       case Token.TRY:
1626 |       case Token.CATCH:
1627 |       case Token.SWITCH:
1628 |       case Token.CASE:
1629 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
1630 |         return true;
1631 |       default:
1632 |         return false;
1633 |     }
1634 |   }

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

===== M0209 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0220 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

===== M0221 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 242-305
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
242 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
243 |     this.options = options;
244 |     if (errorManager == null) {
245 |       if (outStream == null) {
246 |         setErrorManager(
247 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
248 |       } else {
249 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
250 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
251 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
252 |         setErrorManager(printer);
253 |       }
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
257 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
258 |       options.checkTypes = true;
259 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
260 |       options.checkTypes = false;
261 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
262 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
263 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
264 |       // parser type warnings.
265 |       options.setWarningLevel(
266 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
267 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
268 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
269 |     }
270 | 
271 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
272 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
273 |       options.setWarningLevel(
274 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
275 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
276 |     }
277 | 
278 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
279 |       options.setWarningLevel(
280 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
281 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
282 |     }
283 | 
284 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
285 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
286 |     guards.add(
287 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
288 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
289 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
290 | 
291 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
292 | 
293 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
294 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
295 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
296 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
297 |     // error off.
298 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
299 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
300 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
301 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
302 |     }
303 | 
304 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
305 |   }

449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0243 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 |     compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(acceptEs5);
126 | 
127 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
128 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
129 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

279 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
280 |     switch (c) {
281 |       case '@':
282 |       case '*':
283 |       case ',':
284 |       case '>':
285 |       case ':':
286 |       case '(':

===== M0247 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3003-3029
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3003 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3004 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3005 |     if (info == null) {
3006 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3007 |       if (parent == null) {
3008 |         return null;
3009 |       }
3010 | 
3011 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3012 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3013 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3014 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3015 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3016 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3017 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3018 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3019 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3020 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3021 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3022 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3023 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3024 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3025 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3026 |       }
3027 |     }
3028 |     return info;
3029 |   }

===== M0252 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 460-475
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
460 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
461 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
462 |       Set<String> directives = null;
463 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
464 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
465 |         if (directives == null) {
466 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
467 |         } else {
468 |           directives.add(directive);
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       if (directives != null) {
473 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
474 |       }
475 |     }

1517 |       public String call() throws Exception {

===== M0286 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 399-412
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
399 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
400 |       Node ret;
401 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
402 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
403 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
404 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
405 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
406 |       } else {
407 |         ret = transform(n);
408 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
409 |       }
410 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
411 |       return ret;
412 |     }

===== M0288 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

===== M0292 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1719-1745
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1719 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1720 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1721 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1722 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1723 |       skipEOLs();
1724 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1725 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1726 |       skipEOLs();
1727 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1728 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1729 |       skipEOLs();
1730 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1731 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1732 |       String string = stream.getString();
1733 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1734 |         skipEOLs();
1735 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1736 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1737 |         return newStringNode(string);
1738 |       } else {
1739 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1740 |       }
1741 |     }
1742 | 
1743 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1744 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1745 |   }

   * Gets the number of properties of this object.
   */
Method code:
151 |   @Override
152 |   public int getPropertiesCount() {
153 |     ObjectType implicitPrototype = getImplicitPrototype();
154 |     if (implicitPrototype == null) {
155 |       return this.properties.size();
156 |     }

===== M0300 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefaultPassConfig.getMainOptimizationLoop()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefaultPassConfig.java
Original range: 676-705
Documentation:
/** Creates the passes for the main optimization loop. */
Method code:
676 |   private List<PassFactory> getMainOptimizationLoop() {
677 |     List<PassFactory> passes = Lists.newArrayList();
678 |     if (options.inlineGetters) {
679 |       passes.add(inlineSimpleMethods);
680 |     }
681 | 
682 |     passes.addAll(getCodeRemovingPasses());
683 | 
684 |     if (options.inlineFunctions || options.inlineLocalFunctions) {
685 |       passes.add(inlineFunctions);
686 |     }
687 | 
688 |     boolean runOptimizeCalls = options.optimizeCalls
689 |         || options.optimizeParameters
690 |         || options.optimizeReturns;
691 | 
692 |     if (options.removeUnusedVars || options.removeUnusedLocalVars) {
693 |       if (options.deadAssignmentElimination) {
694 |         passes.add(deadAssignmentsElimination);
695 |       }
696 |       if (!runOptimizeCalls) {
697 |         passes.add(removeUnusedVars);
698 |       }
699 |     }
700 |     if (runOptimizeCalls) {
701 |       passes.add(optimizeCallsAndRemoveUnusedVars);
702 |     }
703 |     assertAllLoopablePasses(passes);
704 |     return passes;
705 |   }

83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
86 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {

92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }

97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {

100 |     }

103 |     // the source file and module.

234 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

===== M0354 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1672-1713
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1672 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1673 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1674 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1675 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1676 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1677 |       // {?} - right curly
1678 |       // {?=} - equals
1679 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1680 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1681 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1682 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1683 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1684 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1685 |       token = next();
1686 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1687 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1688 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1689 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1690 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1691 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1692 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1693 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1694 |       }
1695 | 
1696 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1697 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1698 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1699 |     } else {
1700 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1701 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1702 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1703 |           next();
1704 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1705 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1706 |           next();
1707 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1708 |         }
1709 |       }
1710 | 
1711 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1712 |     }
1713 |   }

===== M0358 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CreateSyntheticBlocks.Callback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CreateSyntheticBlocks.java
Original range: 169-215
Documentation:

Method code:
169 |     @Override
170 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
171 |       if (!n.isCall() || !n.getFirstChild().isName()) {
172 |         return;
173 |       }
174 | 
175 |       Node callTarget = n.getFirstChild();
176 |       String callName = callTarget.getString();
177 | 
178 |       if (startMarkerName.equals(callName)) {
179 |         if (!parent.isExprResult()) {
180 |           compiler.report(
181 |               t.makeError(n, INVALID_MARKER_USAGE, startMarkerName));
182 |           return;
183 |         }
184 |         markerStack.push(parent);
185 |         return;
186 |       }
187 | 
188 |       if (!endMarkerName.equals(callName)) {
189 |         return;
190 |       }
191 | 
192 |       Node endMarkerNode = parent;
193 |       if (!endMarkerNode.isExprResult()) {
194 |         compiler.report(
195 |             t.makeError(n, INVALID_MARKER_USAGE, endMarkerName));
196 |         return;
197 |       }
198 | 
199 |       if (markerStack.isEmpty()) {
200 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n, UNMATCHED_END_MARKER,
201 |             startMarkerName, endMarkerName));
202 |         return;
203 |       }
204 | 
205 |       Node startMarkerNode = markerStack.pop();
206 |       if (endMarkerNode.getParent() != startMarkerNode.getParent()) {
207 |         // The end marker isn't in the same block as the start marker.
208 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n, UNMATCHED_END_MARKER,
209 |             startMarkerName, endMarkerName));
210 |         return;
211 |       }
212 | 
213 |       // This is a valid marker set add it to the list of markers to process.
214 |       validMarkers.add(new Marker(startMarkerNode, endMarkerNode));
215 |     }

===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.CollectDefines.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 286-371
Documentation:

Method code:
286 |     @Override
287 |     public  void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
288 |       RefInfo refInfo = allRefInfo.get(n);
289 |       if (refInfo != null) {
290 |         Ref ref = refInfo.ref;
291 |         Name name = refInfo.name;
292 |         String fullName = name.getFullName();
293 |         switch (ref.type) {
294 |           case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
295 |           case SET_FROM_LOCAL:
296 |             Node valParent = getValueParent(ref);
297 |             Node val = valParent.getLastChild();
298 |             if (valParent.isAssign() && name.isSimpleName() &&
299 |                 name.getDeclaration() == ref) {
300 |               // For defines, it's an error if a simple name is assigned
301 |               // before it's declared
302 |               compiler.report(
303 |                   t.makeError(val, INVALID_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, fullName));
304 |             } else if (processDefineAssignment(t, fullName, val, valParent)) {
305 |               // remove the assignment so that the variable is still declared,
306 |               // but no longer assigned to a value, e.g.,
307 |               // DEF_FOO = 5; // becomes "5;"
308 | 
309 |               // We can't remove the ASSIGN/VAR when we're still visiting its
310 |               // children, so we'll have to come back later to remove it.
311 |               refInfo.name.removeRef(ref);
312 |               lvalueToRemoveLater = valParent;
313 |             }
314 |             break;
315 |           default:
316 |             if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
317 |               // Treat this as a reference to a define in the global scope.
318 |               // After this point, the define must not be reassigned,
319 |               // or it's an error.
320 |               DefineInfo info = assignableDefines.get(fullName);
321 |               if (info != null) {
322 |                 setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
323 |                 assignableDefines.remove(fullName);
324 |               }
325 |             }
326 |             break;
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 | 
330 |       if (!t.inGlobalScope() &&
331 |           n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isDefine()) {
332 |         // warn about @define annotations in local scopes
333 |         compiler.report(
334 |             t.makeError(n, NON_GLOBAL_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, ""));
335 |       }
336 | 
337 |       if (lvalueToRemoveLater == n) {
338 |         lvalueToRemoveLater = null;
339 |         if (n.isAssign()) {
340 |           Node last = n.getLastChild();
341 |           n.removeChild(last);
342 |           parent.replaceChild(n, last);
343 |         } else {
344 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.isName());
345 |           n.removeChild(n.getFirstChild());
346 |         }
347 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
348 |       }
349 | 
350 |       if (n.isCall()) {
351 |         if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
352 |           // If there's a function call in the global scope,
353 |           // we just say it's unsafe and freeze all the defines.
354 |           //
355 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): We could be a lot smarter here. For example,
356 |           // ReplaceOverriddenVars keeps a call graph of all functions and
357 |           // which functions/variables that they reference, and tries
358 |           // to statically determine which functions are "safe" and which
359 |           // are not. But this would be overkill, expecially because
360 |           // the intended use of defines is with config_files, where
361 |           // all the defines are at the top of the bundle.
362 |           for (DefineInfo info : assignableDefines.values()) {
363 |             setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
364 |           }
365 | 
366 |           assignableDefines.clear();
367 |         }
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       updateAssignAllowedStack(n, false);
371 |     }

===== M0385 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken,WhitespaceOption)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1328-1429
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the text found on the current line and all subsequent
   * until either an annotation, end of comment or end of file is reached.
   * Note that if this method detects an end of line as the first token, it
   * will quit immediately (indicating that there is no text where it was
   * expected).  Note that token = info.token; should be called after this
   * method is used to update the token properly in the parser.
   *
   * @param token The start token.
   * @param option How to handle whitespace.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1328 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
1329 |   private ExtractionInfo extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken token,
1330 |                                                       WhitespaceOption option) {
1331 | 
1332 |     if (token == JsDocToken.EOC || token == JsDocToken.EOL ||
1333 |         token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
1334 |       return new ExtractionInfo("", token);
1335 |     }
1336 | 
1337 |     stream.update();
1338 |     int startLineno = stream.getLineno();
1339 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno() + 1;
1340 | 
1341 |     // Read the content from the first line.
1342 |     String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1343 |     if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1344 |       line = line.trim();
1345 |     }
1346 | 
1347 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1348 |     builder.append(line);
1349 | 
1350 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1351 |     token = next();
1352 | 
1353 |     boolean ignoreStar = false;
1354 | 
1355 |     // Track the start of the line to count whitespace that
1356 |     // the tokenizer skipped. Because this case is rare, it's easier
1357 |     // to do this here than in the tokenizer.
1358 | 
1359 |     do {
1360 |       switch (token) {
1361 |         case STAR:
1362 |           if (ignoreStar) {
1363 |             // Mark the position after the star as the new start of the line.
1364 |           } else {
1365 |             // The star is part of the comment.
1366 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1367 |               builder.append(' ');
1368 |             }
1369 | 
1370 |             builder.append('*');
1371 |           }
1372 | 
1373 |           token = next();
1374 |           continue;
1375 | 
1376 |         case EOL:
1377 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.SINGLE_LINE) {
1378 |             builder.append("\n");
1379 |           }
1380 | 
1381 |           ignoreStar = true;
1382 |           token = next();
1383 |           continue;
1384 | 
1385 |         default:
1386 |           ignoreStar = false;
1387 |           state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1388 | 
1389 |               // All tokens must be separated by a space.
1390 | 
1391 |           if (token == JsDocToken.EOC ||
1392 |               token == JsDocToken.EOF ||
1393 |               // When we're capturing a license block, annotations
1394 |               // in the block are ok.
1395 |               (token == JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
1396 |                option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE)) {
1397 |             String multilineText = builder.toString();
1398 | 
1399 |             if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1400 |               multilineText = multilineText.trim();
1401 |             }
1402 | 
1403 |             int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1404 |             int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1405 | 
1406 |             if (multilineText.length() > 0) {
1407 |               jsdocBuilder.markText(multilineText, startLineno, startCharno,
1408 |                   endLineno, endCharno);
1409 |             }
1410 | 
1411 |             return new ExtractionInfo(multilineText, token);
1412 |           }
1413 | 
1414 |           if (builder.length() > 0) {
1415 |             builder.append(' ');
1416 |           }
1417 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1418 | 
1419 |           line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1420 | 
1421 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1422 |             line = trimEnd(line);
1423 |           }
1424 | 
1425 |           builder.append(line);
1426 |           token = next();
1427 |       }
1428 |     } while (true);
1429 |   }
