===== M0403 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1216-1227
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1216 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1217 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1218 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1219 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1220 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1221 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1222 |       } else {
1223 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1224 |       }
1225 |     }
1226 |     return builder.build();
1227 |   }

===== M0404 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.opToStr(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2123-2168
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.
   *
   * @param operator the operator's token value to convert
   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator
   */
Method code:
2123 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
2124 |     switch (operator) {
2125 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
2126 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
2127 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
2128 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";
2129 |       case Token.BITAND: return "&";
2130 |       case Token.SHEQ: return "===";
2131 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
2132 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
2133 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
2134 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
2135 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
2136 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
2137 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
2138 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
2139 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
2140 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
2141 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
2142 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
2143 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";
2144 |       case Token.DIV: return "/";
2145 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
2146 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
2147 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
2148 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
2149 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
2150 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
2151 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";
2152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR: return "|=";
2153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR: return "^=";
2154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";
2155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH: return "<<=";
2156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH: return ">>=";
2157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH: return ">>>=";
2158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD: return "+=";
2159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB: return "-=";
2160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL: return "*=";
2161 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV: return "/=";
2162 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD: return "%=";
2163 |       case Token.VOID: return "void";
2164 |       case Token.TYPEOF: return "typeof";
2165 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF: return "instanceof";
2166 |       default: return null;
2167 |     }
2168 |   }

===== M0429 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReplaceIdGenerators.GatherGenerators.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReplaceIdGenerators.java
Original range: 77-116
Documentation:

Method code:
77 |     @Override
78 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
79 |       JSDocInfo doc = n.getJSDocInfo();
80 |       if (doc == null) {
81 |         return;
82 |       }
83 | 
84 |       if (!doc.isConsistentIdGenerator() &&
85 |           !doc.isIdGenerator()) {
86 |         return;
87 |       }
88 | 
89 |       if (doc.isConsistentIdGenerator() && doc.isIdGenerator()) {
90 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_GENERATOR_TYPE));
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       String name = null;
94 |       if (n.isAssign()) {
95 |         name = n.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
96 |       } else if (n.isVar()) {
97 |         name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
98 |       } else if (n.isFunction()){
99 |         name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
100 |         if (name.isEmpty()) {
101 |           return;
102 |         }
103 |       }
104 | 
105 |       // TODO(user): Error on function that has both. Or redeclartion
106 |       // on the same function.
107 |       if (doc.isConsistentIdGenerator()) {
108 |         consistNameGenerators.put(name,
109 |             new NameGenerator(Collections.<String>emptySet(), "", null));
110 |         consistNameMap.put(name, Maps.<String, String>newLinkedHashMap());
111 |       } else {
112 |         nameGenerators.put(name,
113 |           new NameGenerator(Collections.<String>emptySet(), "", null));
114 |       }
115 |       idGeneratorMaps.put(name, Lists.<Replacement>newArrayList());
116 |     }

===== M0431 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.runCallable(Callable<T>,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 606-658
Documentation:

Method code:
606 |   @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
607 |   static <T> T runCallable(
608 |       final Callable<T> callable, boolean useLargeStackThread, boolean trace) {
609 | 
610 |     // Under JRE 1.6, the jscompiler overflows the stack when running on some
611 |     // large or complex js code. Here we start a new thread with a larger
612 |     // stack in order to let the compiler do its thing, without having to
613 |     // increase the stack size for *every* thread (which is what -Xss does).
614 |     // Might want to add thread pool support for clients that compile a lot.
615 | 
616 |     final boolean dumpTraceReport = trace;
617 |     final Object[] result = new Object[1];
618 |     final Throwable[] exception = new Throwable[1];
619 |     Runnable runnable = new Runnable() {
620 |       @Override
621 |       public void run() {
622 |         try {
623 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
624 |             Tracer.initCurrentThreadTrace();
625 |           }
626 |           result[0] = callable.call();
627 |         } catch (Throwable e) {
628 |           exception[0] = e;
629 |         } finally {
630 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
631 |             Tracer.logAndClearCurrentThreadTrace();
632 |           }
633 |         }
634 |       }
635 |     };
636 | 
637 |     if (useLargeStackThread) {
638 |       Thread th = new Thread(null, runnable, "jscompiler", COMPILER_STACK_SIZE);
639 |       th.start();
640 |       while (true) {
641 |         try {
642 |           th.join();
643 |           break;
644 |         } catch (InterruptedException ignore) {
645 |           // ignore
646 |         }
647 |       }
648 |     } else {
649 |       runnable.run();
650 |     }
651 | 
652 |     // Pass on any exception caught by the runnable object.
653 |     if (exception[0] != null) {
654 |       throw new RuntimeException(exception[0]);
655 |     }
656 | 
657 |     return (T) result[0];
658 |   }

===== M0439 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.ContextualRenameInverter.exitScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 313-334
Documentation:
/**
     * Rename vars for the current scope, and merge any referenced
     * names into the parent scope reference set.
     */
Method code:
313 |     @Override
314 |     public void exitScope(NodeTraversal t) {
315 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
316 |         return;
317 |       }
318 | 
319 |       for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
320 |         Var v = it.next();
321 |         handleScopeVar(v);
322 |       }
323 | 
324 |       // Merge any names that were referenced but not declared in the current
325 |       // scope.
326 |       Set<String> current = referencedNames;
327 |       referencedNames = referenceStack.pop();
328 |       // If there isn't anything left in the stack we will be going into the
329 |       // global scope: don't try to build a set of referenced names for the
330 |       // global scope.
331 |       if (!referenceStack.isEmpty()) {
332 |         referencedNames.addAll(current);
333 |       }
334 |     }

===== M0445 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 617-634
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
617 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
618 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
619 |         next = c.getNext();
620 |         if (c.isVar()) {
621 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
622 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
623 |           }
624 | 
625 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
626 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
627 |             c.removeChild(name);
628 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
629 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
630 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
631 |           }
632 |         }
633 |       }
634 |     }

===== M0455 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.toString(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1172-1231
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a JSDoc token to its string representation.
   */
Method code:
1172 |   private String toString(JsDocToken token) {
1173 |     switch (token) {
1174 |       case ANNOTATION:
1175 |         return "@" + stream.getString();
1176 | 
1177 |       case BANG:
1178 |         return "!";
1179 | 
1180 |       case COMMA:
1181 |         return ",";
1182 | 
1183 |       case COLON:
1184 |         return ":";
1185 | 
1186 |       case GT:
1187 |         return ">";
1188 | 
1189 |       case LB:
1190 |         return "[";
1191 | 
1192 |       case LC:
1193 |         return "{";
1194 | 
1195 |       case LP:
1196 |         return "(";
1197 | 
1198 |       case LT:
1199 |         return ".<";
1200 | 
1201 |       case QMARK:
1202 |         return "?";
1203 | 
1204 |       case PIPE:
1205 |         return "|";
1206 | 
1207 |       case RB:
1208 |         return "]";
1209 | 
1210 |       case RC:
1211 |         return "}";
1212 | 
1213 |       case RP:
1214 |         return ")";
1215 | 
1216 |       case STAR:
1217 |         return "*";
1218 | 
1219 |       case ELLIPSIS:
1220 |         return "...";
1221 | 
1222 |       case EQUALS:
1223 |         return "=";
1224 | 
1225 |       case STRING:

1227 | 
1228 |       default:

1230 |     }

===== M0484 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.consume(List<PassFactory>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 125-146
Documentation:
/**
   * Add the passes generated by the given factories to the compile sequence.
   *
   * Automatically pulls multi-run passes into fixed point loops. If there
   * are 2 or more multi-run passes in a row, they will run together in
   * the same fixed point loop. If A and B are in the same fixed point loop,
   * the loop will continue to run both A and B until both are finished
   * making changes.
   *
   * Other than that, the PhaseOptimizer is free to tweak the order and
   * frequency of multi-run passes in a fixed-point loop.
   */
Method code:
125 |   void consume(List<PassFactory> factories) {
126 |     Loop currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
127 |     boolean isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
128 |     for (PassFactory factory : factories) {
129 |       if (factory.isOneTimePass()) {
130 |         if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
131 |           passes.add(currentLoop);
132 | 
133 |           currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
134 |           isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
135 |         }
136 |         addOneTimePass(factory);
137 |       } else {
138 |         currentLoop.addLoopedPass(factory);
139 |         isCurrentLoopPopulated = true;
140 |       }
141 |     }
142 | 
143 |     if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
144 |       passes.add(currentLoop);
145 |     }
146 |   }

200 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")

222 |       }

===== M0497 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1236-1387
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1236 |   Node parseInputs() {
1237 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1238 | 
1239 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1240 |     // individual file parse trees.
1241 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1242 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1243 |     }
1244 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1245 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1246 |     }
1247 | 
1248 |     // Parse main js sources.
1249 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1250 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1251 | 
1252 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1253 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1254 | 
1255 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1256 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1257 | 
1258 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1259 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1260 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1261 |     }
1262 | 
1263 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1264 | 
1265 |     try {
1266 |       // Parse externs sources.
1267 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1268 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1269 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1270 |           return null;
1271 |         }
1272 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1273 |       }
1274 | 
1275 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1276 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1277 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1278 |       }
1279 | 
1280 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1281 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1282 | 
1283 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1284 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1285 |           !options.skipAllPasses &&
1286 |           options.closurePass) {
1287 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1288 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1289 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1290 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1291 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1292 |           }
1293 |         }
1294 | 
1295 |         try {
1296 |           inputs =
1297 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1298 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1299 |           staleInputs = true;
1300 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1301 |           report(JSError.make(
1302 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1303 | 
1304 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1305 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1306 |             return null;
1307 |           }
1308 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1309 |           report(JSError.make(
1310 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1311 | 
1312 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1313 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1314 |             return null;
1315 |           }
1316 |         }
1317 |       }
1318 | 
1319 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1320 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1321 | 
1322 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1323 |         if (n == null) {
1324 |           continue;
1325 |         }
1326 | 
1327 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1328 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1329 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1330 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1331 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1332 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1333 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1334 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1335 | 
1336 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1337 | 
1338 |             externs.add(input);
1339 |             staleInputs = true;
1340 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1341 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1342 |             staleInputs = true;
1343 |           }
1344 |         }
1345 |       }
1346 | 
1347 |       if (staleInputs) {
1348 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1349 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1350 |       }
1351 | 
1352 |       // Build the AST.
1353 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1354 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1355 |         if (n == null) {
1356 |           continue;
1357 |         }
1358 | 
1359 |         if (devMode) {
1360 |           runSanityCheck();
1361 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1362 |             return null;
1363 |           }
1364 |         }
1365 | 
1366 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1367 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1368 | 
1369 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1370 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1371 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1372 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1373 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1374 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1375 |         }
1376 | 
1377 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1378 |       }
1379 | 
1380 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1381 |         return null;
1382 |       }
1383 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1384 |     } finally {
1385 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1386 |     }
1387 |   }

===== M0499 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.compileInternal()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 660-705
Documentation:

Method code:
660 |   private void compileInternal() {
661 |     setProgress(0.0);
662 |     parse();
663 |     // 15 percent of the work is assumed to be for parsing (based on some
664 |     // minimal analysis on big JS projects, of course this depends on options)
665 |     setProgress(0.15);
666 |     if (hasErrors()) {
667 |       return;
668 |     }
669 | 
670 |     if (!precheck()) {
671 |       return;
672 |     }
673 | 
674 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
675 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Move this into an instrument() phase maybe?
676 |       check();
677 |       return;
678 |     }
679 | 
680 |     if (!options.skipAllPasses) {
681 |       check();
682 |       if (hasErrors()) {
683 |         return;
684 |       }
685 | 
686 |       if (options.isExternExportsEnabled()
687 |           || options.externExportsPath != null) {
688 |         externExports();
689 |       }
690 | 
691 |       // IDE-mode is defined to stop here, before the heavy rewriting begins.
692 |       if (!options.ideMode) {
693 |         optimize();
694 |       }
695 |     }
696 | 
697 |     if (options.recordFunctionInformation) {
698 |       recordFunctionInformation();
699 |     }
700 | 
701 |     if (options.devMode == DevMode.START_AND_END) {
702 |       runSanityCheck();
703 |     }
704 |     setProgress(1.0);
705 |   }

===== M0501 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

===== M0527 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1623-1639
Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1623 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1624 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1625 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1626 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1627 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1628 |         next();
1629 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1630 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1631 |           next();
1632 |         }
1633 |         skipEOLs();
1634 |         token = next();
1635 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1636 |       }
1637 |     }
1638 |     return typeExpr;
1639 |   }

120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:

135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:

164 |       case Token.DELPROP:

167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

===== M0547 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.CodeBuilder.append(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1738-1757
Documentation:
/** Appends the given string to the text buffer. */
Method code:
1738 |     CodeBuilder append(String str) {
1739 |       sb.append(str);
1740 | 
1741 |       // Adjust the line and column information for the new text.
1742 |       int index = -1;
1743 |       int lastIndex = index;
1744 |       while ((index = str.indexOf('\n', index + 1)) >= 0) {
1745 |         ++lineCount;
1746 |         lastIndex = index;
1747 |       }
1748 | 
1749 |       if (lastIndex == -1) {
1750 |         // No new lines, append the new characters added.
1751 |         colCount += str.length();
1752 |       } else {
1753 |         colCount = str.length() - (lastIndex + 1);
1754 |       }
1755 | 
1756 |       return this;
1757 |     }

/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
415 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
416 |       Preconditions.checkState(
417 |           n.isName()
418 |           || n.isString()
419 |           || n.isGetterDef()
420 |           || n.isSetterDef());
421 | 
422 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
423 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
424 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
425 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent);
426 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
427 |           (parent.isGetProp() &&
428 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
429 |       if (n.isName() || isProperty) {
430 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
431 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
432 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
433 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
434 |           if (assertOnChange) {
435 |             String name = n.getString();
436 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
437 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
438 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
439 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
440 |           }
441 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
442 |         }
443 |       }
444 |     }

Original range: 309-316
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
310 |     if (c <= 127) {
311 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
312 |     } else {

314 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
315 |     }

===== M0566 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.collect(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 302-430
Documentation:

Method code:
302 |     public void collect(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
303 |       if (nodeFilter != null && !nodeFilter.apply(n)) {
304 |         return;
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       // If we are traversing the externs, then we save a pointer to the scope
308 |       // generated by them, so that we can do lookups in it later.
309 |       if (externsRoot != null && n == externsRoot) {
310 |         externsScope = t.getScope();
311 |       }
312 | 
313 |       String name;
314 |       boolean isSet = false;
315 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
316 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
317 | 
318 |       switch (n.getType()) {
319 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
320 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
321 |         case Token.STRING:
322 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
323 |           name = null;
324 |           if (parent != null && parent.isObjectLit()) {
325 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
326 |           }
327 |           if (name == null) return;
328 |           isSet = true;
329 |           switch (n.getType()) {
330 |             case Token.STRING:
331 |               type = getValueType(n.getFirstChild());
332 |               break;
333 |             case Token.GETTER_DEF:
334 |               type = Name.Type.GET;
335 |               break;
336 |             case Token.SETTER_DEF:
337 |               type = Name.Type.SET;
338 |               break;
339 |             default:
340 |               throw new IllegalStateException("unexpected:" + n);
341 |           }
342 |           break;
343 |         case Token.NAME:
344 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
345 |           if (parent != null) {
346 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
347 |               case Token.VAR:
348 |                 isSet = true;
349 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
350 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
351 |                 break;
352 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
353 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
354 |                   isSet = true;
355 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
356 |                 }
357 |                 break;
358 |               case Token.GETPROP:
359 |                 return;
360 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
361 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
362 |                 if (gramps == null ||
363 |                     NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) return;
364 |                 isSet = true;
365 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
366 |                 break;
367 |               case Token.INC:
368 |               case Token.DEC:
369 |                 isSet = true;
370 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
371 |                 break;
372 |               default:
373 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
374 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
375 |                   isSet = true;
376 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
377 |                 }
378 |             }
379 |           }
380 |           name = n.getString();
381 |           break;
382 |         case Token.GETPROP:
383 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
384 |           if (parent != null) {
385 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
386 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
387 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
388 |                   isSet = true;
389 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
390 |                   isPropAssign = true;
391 |                 }
392 |                 break;
393 |               case Token.INC:
394 |               case Token.DEC:
395 |                 isSet = true;
396 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
397 |                 break;
398 |               case Token.GETPROP:
399 |                 return;
400 |               default:
401 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
402 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
403 |                   isSet = true;
404 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
405 |                 }
406 |             }
407 |           }
408 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
409 |           if (name == null) return;
410 |           break;
411 |         default:
412 |           return;
413 |       }
414 | 
415 |       // We are only interested in global names.
416 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
417 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
418 |         return;
419 |       }
420 | 
421 |       if (isSet) {
422 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
423 |           handleSetFromGlobal(t, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
424 |         } else {
425 |           handleSetFromLocal(t, n, parent, name);
426 |         }
427 |       } else {
428 |         handleGet(t, n, parent, name);
429 |       }
430 |     }

===== M0568 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 177-196
Documentation:
/**
   * Run all the passes in the optimizer.
   */
Method code:
177 |   @Override
178 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
179 |     double progress = 0.0;
180 |     double progressStep = 0.0;
181 |     if (progressRange != null) {
182 |       progressStep = (progressRange.maxValue - progressRange.initialValue)
183 |           / passes.size();
184 |       progress = progressRange.initialValue;
185 |     }
186 |     for (CompilerPass pass : passes) {
187 |       pass.process(externs, root);
188 |       if (progressRange != null) {
189 |         progress += progressStep;
190 |         compiler.setProgress(progress);
191 |       }
192 |       if (hasHaltingErrors()) {
193 |         return;
194 |       }
195 |     }
196 |   }

/**
   * Looks for a parameter type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @param lineno The line of the type expression.
   * @param startCharno The starting character position of the type expression.
   * @param matchingLC Whether the type expression starts with a "{".
   * @param onlyParseSimpleNames If true, only simple type names are parsed
   *     (via a call to parseTypeNameAnnotation instead of
   *     parseTypeExpressionAnnotation).
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1142 |   private Node parseAndRecordTypeNode(JsDocToken token, int lineno,
1143 |                                       int startCharno,
1144 |                                       boolean matchingLC,
1145 |                                       boolean onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1146 |     Node typeNode = null;
1147 | 
1148 |     if (onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1149 |       typeNode = parseTypeNameAnnotation(token);
1150 |     } else {
1151 |       typeNode = parseTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1152 |     }
1153 | 
1154 |     if (typeNode != null && !matchingLC) {
1155 |       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
1156 |     }
1157 | 
1158 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1159 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1160 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1161 | 
1162 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(
1163 |           typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endLineno, endCharno, matchingLC);
1164 |     }
1165 | 
1166 |     return typeNode;
1167 |   }

     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.

Method code:
379 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {
380 |         final String defaultResource
381 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
382 | 
383 |         Locale locale = Locale.getDefault();
384 | 
385 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
386 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
387 | 
388 |         String formatString;
389 |         try {

394 |         }

Method code:
87 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
88 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
89 |       ComposeWarningsGuard composeGuard = (ComposeWarningsGuard) guard;
90 |       if (composeGuard.demoteErrors) {
91 |         this.demoteErrors = composeGuard.demoteErrors;
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
95 |       addGuards(Lists.newArrayList(composeGuard.guards.descendingSet()));
96 |     } else {

===== M0613 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 120-159
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   @Override
121 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
122 |     new NodeTraversal(
123 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
124 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
125 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
126 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
127 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
128 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
129 |     }
130 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
131 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
132 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
133 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
134 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
135 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
136 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
137 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
138 |     //   function f() {
139 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
140 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
141 |     //   }
142 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
143 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
144 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
145 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
146 |         .process(externs, root);
147 | 
148 |     FindExposeAnnotations findExposeAnnotations = new FindExposeAnnotations();
149 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, findExposeAnnotations);
150 |     if (!findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties.isEmpty()) {
151 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
152 |           new RewriteExposedProperties(
153 |               findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties));
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
157 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
158 |     }
159 |   }

===== M0619 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.CollectDefines.CollectDefines(AbstractCompiler,List<Name>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 237-265
Documentation:

Method code:
237 |     CollectDefines(AbstractCompiler compiler, List<Name> listOfDefines) {
238 |       this.compiler = compiler;
239 |       this.allDefines = Maps.newHashMap();
240 | 
241 |       assignableDefines = Maps.newHashMap();
242 |       assignAllowed = new ArrayDeque<Integer>();
243 |       assignAllowed.push(1);
244 | 
245 |       // Create a map of references to defines keyed by node for easy lookup
246 |       allRefInfo = Maps.newHashMap();
247 |       for (Name name : listOfDefines) {
248 |         Ref decl = name.getDeclaration();
249 |         if (decl != null) {
250 |           allRefInfo.put(decl.node,
251 |                          new RefInfo(decl, name));
252 |         }
253 |         for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
254 |           if (ref == decl) {
255 |             // Declarations were handled above.
256 |             continue;
257 |           }
258 | 
259 |           // If there's a TWIN def, only put one of the twins in.
260 |           if (ref.getTwin() == null || !ref.getTwin().isSet()) {
261 |             allRefInfo.put(ref.node, new RefInfo(ref, name));
262 |           }
263 |         }
264 |       }
265 |     }

===== M0627 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CompilerOptions.getReplacementsHelper(Map<String, Object>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CompilerOptions.java
Original range: 931-949
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a map of String->Node from a map of String->Number/String/Boolean.
   */
Method code:
931 |   private static Map<String, Node> getReplacementsHelper(
932 |       Map<String, Object> source) {
933 |     Map<String, Node> map = Maps.newHashMap();
934 |     for (Map.Entry<String, Object> entry : source.entrySet()) {
935 |       String name = entry.getKey();
936 |       Object value = entry.getValue();
937 |       if (value instanceof Boolean) {
938 |         map.put(name, NodeUtil.booleanNode(((Boolean) value).booleanValue()));
939 |       } else if (value instanceof Integer) {
940 |         map.put(name, IR.number(((Integer) value).intValue()));
941 |       } else if (value instanceof Double) {
942 |         map.put(name, IR.number(((Double) value).doubleValue()));
943 |       } else {
944 |         Preconditions.checkState(value instanceof String);
945 |         map.put(name, IR.string((String) value));
946 |       }
947 |     }
948 |     return map;
949 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.

Method code:
466 |   private static boolean mayBeStatement(Node n) {
467 |     switch (n.getType()) {
468 |       case Token.EMPTY:
469 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
470 |         // EMPTY and FUNCTION are used both in expression and statement
471 |         // contexts
472 |         return true;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.BLOCK:
475 |       case Token.BREAK:
476 |       case Token.CONST:
477 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
478 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
479 |       case Token.DO:
480 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
481 |       case Token.FOR:
482 |       case Token.IF:
483 |       case Token.LABEL:
484 |       case Token.RETURN:
485 |       case Token.SWITCH:
486 |       case Token.THROW:
487 |       case Token.TRY:
488 |       case Token.VAR:
489 |       case Token.WHILE:
490 |       case Token.WITH:
491 |         return true;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         return false;
495 |     }
496 |   }

===== M0651 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.VarCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/VarCheck.java
Original range: 120-203
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   @Override
121 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
122 |     if (!n.isName()) {
123 |       return;
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     String varName = n.getString();
127 | 
128 |     // Only a function can have an empty name.
129 |     if (varName.isEmpty()) {
130 |       Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
131 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent));
132 |       return;
133 |     }
134 | 
135 |     // Check if this is a declaration for a var that has been declared
136 |     // elsewhere. If so, mark it as a duplicate.
137 |     if ((parent.isVar() ||
138 |          NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent)) &&
139 |         varsToDeclareInExterns.contains(varName)) {
140 |       createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
141 | 
142 |       n.addSuppression("duplicate");
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     // Check that the var has been declared.
146 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
147 |     Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(varName);
148 |     if (var == null) {
149 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
150 |         // e.g. [ function foo() {} ], it's okay if "foo" isn't defined in the
151 |         // current scope.
152 |       } else {
153 |         // The extern checks are stricter, don't report a second error.
154 |         if (!strictExternCheck || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
155 |           t.report(n, UNDEFINED_VAR_ERROR, varName);
156 |         }
157 | 
158 |         if (sanityCheck) {
159 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected variable " + varName);
160 |         } else {
161 |           createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
162 |           scope.getGlobalScope().declare(varName, n,
163 |               null, getSynthesizedExternsInput());
164 |         }
165 |       }
166 |       return;
167 |     }
168 | 
169 |     CompilerInput currInput = t.getInput();
170 |     CompilerInput varInput = var.input;
171 |     if (currInput == varInput || currInput == null || varInput == null) {
172 |       // The variable was defined in the same file. This is fine.
173 |       return;
174 |     }
175 | 
176 |     // Check module dependencies.
177 |     JSModule currModule = currInput.getModule();
178 |     JSModule varModule = varInput.getModule();
179 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
180 |     if (!sanityCheck &&
181 |         varModule != currModule && varModule != null && currModule != null) {
182 |       if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(currModule, varModule)) {
183 |         // The module dependency was properly declared.
184 |       } else {
185 |         if (scope.isGlobal()) {
186 |           if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(varModule, currModule)) {
187 |             // The variable reference violates a declared module dependency.
188 |             t.report(n, VIOLATED_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
189 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
190 |           } else {
191 |             // The variable reference is between two modules that have no
192 |             // dependency relationship. This should probably be considered an
193 |             // error, but just issue a warning for now.

===== M0669 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 374-417
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
374 |   boolean setPrototype(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
375 |     if (prototype == null) {
376 |       return false;
377 |     }
378 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
379 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
380 |       return false;
381 |     }
382 | 
383 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
384 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
385 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
386 | 
387 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
388 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
389 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
390 | 
391 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
392 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
393 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
394 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
395 |     }
396 | 
397 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
398 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
399 |       if (superClass != null) {
400 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       if (isInterface()) {
404 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
405 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
406 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
407 |           }
408 |         }
409 |       }
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
413 |       clearCachedValues();
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     return true;
417 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 454-470
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
454 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
455 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
456 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
457 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
458 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
459 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {

462 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
463 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
464 |         // will get an error down the line.
465 |         inputs.add(input);
466 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
467 |       }
468 |     }
469 |     return inputs;
470 |   }

===== M0694 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 341-367
Documentation:

Method code:
341 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
342 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
343 |     // two cases:
344 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
345 |     // and
346 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
347 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
348 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
349 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
350 |     //
351 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
352 |     //
353 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
354 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
355 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
356 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
357 |     //
358 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
359 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
360 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
361 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
362 | 
363 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
364 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
365 |     }
366 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
367 |   }

===== M0699 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

/**

   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's

===== M0713 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 560-607
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
560 |     private void extractForInitializer(
561 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
562 | 
563 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
564 |         next = c.getNext();
565 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
566 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
567 |         switch (c.getType()) {
568 |           case Token.LABEL:
569 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
570 |             break;
571 |           case Token.FOR:
572 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
573 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
574 |               if (first.isVar()) {
575 |                 // Transform:
576 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
577 |                 // to:
578 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
579 |                 Node newStatement = first;
580 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
581 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
582 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
583 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
584 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
585 |               }
586 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
587 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
588 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
589 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
590 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
591 | 
592 |               Node newStatement;
593 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
594 |               // but are handled differently.
595 |               if (init.isVar()) {
596 |                 newStatement = init;
597 |               } else {
598 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
599 |               }
600 | 
601 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
602 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
603 |             }
604 |             break;
605 |         }
606 |       }
607 |     }

===== M0714 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseModifiesTag(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1020-1059
Documentation:
/**
   * Parse a {@code @modifies} tag of the form
   * {@code @modifies&#123;this|arguments|param&#125;}.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   */
Method code:
1020 |   private JsDocToken parseModifiesTag(JsDocToken token) {
1021 |     if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1022 |       Set<String> modifies = new HashSet<String>();
1023 |       while (true) {
1024 |         if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1025 |           String name = stream.getString();
1026 |           if (!modifiesAnnotationKeywords.contains(name)
1027 |               && !jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
1028 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.unknown", name,
1029 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1030 |           }
1031 | 
1032 |           modifies.add(stream.getString());
1033 |           token = next();
1034 |         } else {
1035 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
1036 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1037 |           return token;
1038 |         }
1039 | 
1040 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1041 |           token = next();
1042 |         } else {
1043 |           break;
1044 |         }
1045 |       }
1046 | 
1047 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1048 |         parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
1049 |             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1050 |       } else {
1051 |         token = next();
1052 |         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordModifies(modifies)) {
1053 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.duplicate",
1054 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1055 |         }
1056 |       }
1057 |     }
1058 |     return token;
1059 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 616-698
Documentation:

Method code:
616 |     @Override
617 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
618 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
619 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
620 |       if (name == null) {
621 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
622 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
623 |           errorReporter.error(
624 |             "unnamed function statement",
625 |             sourceName,
626 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
627 | 
628 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
629 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
630 |         }
631 |         name = new Name();
632 |         name.setIdentifier("");
633 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
634 |       }
635 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
636 |       Node newName = transform(name);
637 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
638 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
639 |         // declaration.
640 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
641 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
642 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
643 |         // same line as the function keyword.
644 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
645 |             functionNode.getLp();
646 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
647 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
648 |       }
649 | 
650 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
651 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
652 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
653 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
654 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
655 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
656 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
657 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
658 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
659 |       if (fnName != null) {
660 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
661 |       } else {
662 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
663 |       }
664 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
665 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
666 | 
667 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
668 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
669 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
670 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
671 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
672 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
673 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
674 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
675 |         } else {
676 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
677 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
678 |           // (an error has already been reported).
679 |           Preconditions.checkState(
680 |               config.isIdeMode
681 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
682 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
683 |         }
684 |       }
685 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
686 | 
687 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
688 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
689 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
690 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
691 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
692 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
693 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
694 |       }
695 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
696 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
697 |      return node;
698 |     }

===== M0737 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

===== M0738 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

===== M0741 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 513-541
Documentation:

Method code:
513 |     @Override
514 |     void endFile() {
515 |       super.endFile();
516 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
517 |         return;
518 |       }
519 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
520 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
521 |         append(";");
522 |         startNewLine();
523 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
524 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
525 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
526 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
527 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
528 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
529 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
530 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
531 |         lineIndex--;
532 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
533 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
534 |         append(";");
535 |         startNewLine();
536 |       } else {
537 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
538 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
539 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
540 |       }
541 |     }

Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()

Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2333 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2334 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2335 |     int c;
2336 |     while (true) {
2337 |       c = stream.getChar();
2338 |       if (c == ' ') {
2339 |         continue;
2340 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2341 |         matchedLc = true;
2342 |         break;
2343 |       } else {
2344 |         break;
2345 |       }
2346 |     }
2347 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2348 |     return matchedLc;
2349 |   }

===== M0762 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 212-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

75 |   @Override
76 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
77 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
78 |     Renamer renamer;
79 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
80 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
81 |       // be a function.
82 |       Preconditions.checkState(
83 |           !declarationRoot.isFunction() ||
84 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
85 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
86 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
87 |     } else {
88 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     if (!declarationRoot.isFunction()) {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }
