===== M0780 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.toSource(CodeBuilder,int,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1628-1690
Documentation:
/**
   * Writes out js code from a root node. If printing input delimiters, this
   * method will attach a comment to the start of the text indicating which
   * input the output derived from. If there were any preserve annotations
   * within the root's source, they will also be printed in a block comment
   * at the beginning of the output.
   */
Method code:
1628 |   public void toSource(final CodeBuilder cb,
1629 |                        final int inputSeqNum,
1630 |                        final Node root) {
1631 |     runInCompilerThread(new Callable<Void>() {
1632 |       @Override
1633 |       public Void call() throws Exception {
1634 |         if (options.printInputDelimiter) {
1635 |           if ((cb.getLength() > 0) && !cb.endsWith("\n")) {
1636 |             cb.append("\n");  // Make sure that the label starts on a new line
1637 |           }
1638 |           Preconditions.checkState(root.isScript());
1639 | 
1640 |           String delimiter = options.inputDelimiter;
1641 | 
1642 |           String inputName = root.getInputId().getIdName();
1643 |           String sourceName = root.getSourceFileName();
1644 |           Preconditions.checkState(sourceName != null);
1645 |           Preconditions.checkState(!sourceName.isEmpty());
1646 | 
1647 |           delimiter = delimiter
1648 |               .replaceAll("%name%", Matcher.quoteReplacement(inputName))
1649 |               .replaceAll("%num%", String.valueOf(inputSeqNum));
1650 | 
1651 |           cb.append(delimiter)
1652 |             .append("\n");
1653 |         }
1654 |         if (root.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
1655 |             root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null) {
1656 |           cb.append("/*\n")
1657 |             .append(root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense())
1658 |             .append("*/\n");
1659 |         }
1660 | 
1661 |         // If there is a valid source map, then indicate to it that the current
1662 |         // root node's mappings are offset by the given string builder buffer.
1663 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
1664 |           sourceMap.setStartingPosition(
1665 |               cb.getLineIndex(), cb.getColumnIndex());
1666 |         }
1667 | 
1668 |         // if LanguageMode is ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT, only print 'use strict'
1669 |         // for the first input file
1670 |         String code = toSource(root, sourceMap, inputSeqNum == 0);
1671 |         if (!code.isEmpty()) {
1672 |           cb.append(code);
1673 | 
1674 |           // In order to avoid parse ambiguity when files are concatenated
1675 |           // together, all files should end in a semi-colon. Do a quick
1676 |           // heuristic check if there's an obvious semi-colon already there.
1677 |           int length = code.length();
1678 |           char lastChar = code.charAt(length - 1);
1679 |           char secondLastChar = length >= 2 ?
1680 |               code.charAt(length - 2) : '\0';
1681 |           boolean hasSemiColon = lastChar == ';' ||
1682 |               (lastChar == '\n' && secondLastChar == ';');
1683 |           if (!hasSemiColon) {
1684 |             cb.append(";");
1685 |           }
1686 |         }
1687 |         return null;
1688 |       }
1689 |     });
1690 |   }

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

157 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType that) {
158 |     if (this == that) {
159 |       return true;
160 |     } else if (this.isNominalType()) {

162 |       if (thatObj != null && thatObj.isNominalType()) {

85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }

90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
114 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
119 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
120 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
121 |       } else {
122 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
123 |       }
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);

129 |     switch (type) {

131 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
132 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
133 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
134 | 
135 |         add("try");

137 |

139 |         // isn't a catch block

143 |         }

146 |           add("finally");
147 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |       }

152 |       case Token.CATCH:
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
154 |         add("catch(");
155 |         add(first);
156 |         add(")");
157 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
158 |         break;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.THROW:
161 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
162 |         add("throw");
163 |         add(first);
164 | 
165 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
166 |         // parse this.
167 |         cc.endStatement(true);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       case Token.RETURN:
171 |         add("return");
172 |         if (childCount == 1) {
173 |           add(first);
174 |         } else {
175 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
176 |         }
177 |         cc.endStatement();
178 |         break;

180 |       case Token.VAR:
181 |         if (first != null) {

183 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
184 |         }

188 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());

192 |       case Token.NAME:
193 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
194 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
195 |         } else {

197 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());

199 |           if (first.isComma()) {
200 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
201 |           } else {

203 |             // precedence.
204 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
205 |           }

211 |         addArrayList(first);

218 |         add(")");
219 |         break;

222 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);

224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
228 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
229 |         break;

231 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
232 |       case Token.VOID:
233 |       case Token.NOT:
234 |       case Token.BITNOT:
235 |       case Token.POS: {
236 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
237 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
239 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
240 |         break;

245 |

247 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
248 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
249 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {

251 |         } else {

253 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
254 |         }

257 |       }

261 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
262 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);

309 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
310 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:

312 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);

316 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());

318 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
319 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
320 |           add("get ");
321 |         } else {
322 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
323 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());

357 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
358 |         }

360 |         if (preserveBlock) {

362 |         }

365 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||

369 |                 n.getParent().isScript());

371 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);

383 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
384 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {

390 |         }

392 |       }

394 |       case Token.FOR:
395 |         if (childCount == 4) {
396 |           add("for(");
397 |           if (first.isVar()) {
398 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           } else {
400 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           }

404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());

408 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
409 |         } else {
410 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
411 |           add("for(");
412 |           add(first);
413 |           add("in");
414 |           add(first.getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         }
419 |         break;
420 | 
421 |       case Token.DO:
422 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
423 |         add("do");
424 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
425 |         add("while(");
426 |         add(last);
427 |         add(")");
428 |         cc.endStatement();
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.WHILE:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("while(");
434 |         add(first);
435 |         add(")");
436 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
437 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.EMPTY:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
448 |         Preconditions.checkState(
449 |             last.isString(),

451 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
452 |         if (needsParens) {

456 |         if (needsParens) {
457 |           add(")");
458 |         }

461 |         break;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       case Token.GETELEM:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(
466 |             childCount == 2,
467 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
468 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
469 |         add("[");
470 |         add(first.getNext());
471 |         add("]");
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.WITH:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         add("with(");
477 |         add(first);
478 |         add(")");
479 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
480 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.INC:
484 |       case Token.DEC: {
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
486 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
487 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
488 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
489 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
490 |         if (postProp != 0) {

492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |         } else {
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |           add(first);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       case Token.CALL:
501 |         // We have two special cases here:
502 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
503 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
504 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
505 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
506 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
507 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
508 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
509 |         // that must be preserved.
510 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
511 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
512 |           add("(0,");
513 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
514 |           add(")");
515 |         } else {
516 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
517 |         }
518 |         add("(");
519 |         addList(first.getNext());
520 |         add(")");
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.IF:
524 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
525 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
526 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
527 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
528 |           cc.beginBlock();
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         add("if(");
532 |         add(first);
533 |         add(")");
534 | 
535 |         if (hasElse) {
536 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
537 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
538 |           add("else");
539 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
540 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
541 |         } else {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
543 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
544 |         }
545 | 
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.endBlock();
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NULL:
552 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
553 |         add("null");
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.THIS:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add("this");
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.FALSE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
563 |         add("false");
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TRUE:
567 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
568 |         add("true");
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
573 |         add("continue");
574 |         if (childCount == 1) {
575 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
576 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
577 |           }
578 |           add(" ");
579 |           add(first);
580 |         }
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         add("debugger");
587 |         cc.endStatement();
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.BREAK:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("break");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().isObjectLit()) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n);
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isString());
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       default:
726 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
727 |     }
728 | 
729 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
730 |   }

===== M0802 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefaultPassConfig.getOptimizations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefaultPassConfig.java
Original range: 340-673
Documentation:

Method code:
340 |   @Override
341 |   protected List<PassFactory> getOptimizations() {
342 |     List<PassFactory> passes = Lists.newArrayList();
343 |     passes.add(garbageCollectChecks);
344 | 
345 |     // TODO(nicksantos): The order of these passes makes no sense, and needs
346 |     // to be re-arranged.
347 | 
348 |     if (options.runtimeTypeCheck) {
349 |       passes.add(runtimeTypeCheck);
350 |     }
351 | 
352 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeStandardOptimizations"));
353 | 
354 |     if (options.replaceIdGenerators) {
355 |       passes.add(replaceIdGenerators);
356 |     }
357 | 
358 |     // Optimizes references to the arguments variable.
359 |     if (options.optimizeArgumentsArray) {
360 |       passes.add(optimizeArgumentsArray);
361 |     }
362 | 
363 |     // Abstract method removal works best on minimally modified code, and also
364 |     // only needs to run once.
365 |     if (options.closurePass &&
366 |         (options.removeAbstractMethods || options.removeClosureAsserts)) {
367 |       passes.add(closureCodeRemoval);
368 |     }
369 | 
370 |     // Collapsing properties can undo constant inlining, so we do this before
371 |     // the main optimization loop.
372 |     if (options.collapseProperties) {
373 |       passes.add(collapseProperties);
374 |     }
375 | 
376 |     // ReplaceStrings runs after CollapseProperties in order to simplify
377 |     // pulling in values of constants defined in enums structures.
378 |     if (!options.replaceStringsFunctionDescriptions.isEmpty()) {
379 |       passes.add(replaceStrings);
380 |     }
381 | 
382 |     // Tighten types based on actual usage.
383 |     if (options.tightenTypes) {
384 |       passes.add(tightenTypesBuilder);
385 |     }
386 | 
387 |     // Property disambiguation should only run once and needs to be done
388 |     // soon after type checking, both so that it can make use of type
389 |     // information and so that other passes can take advantage of the renamed
390 |     // properties.
391 |     if (options.disambiguateProperties) {
392 |       passes.add(disambiguateProperties);
393 |     }
394 | 
395 |     if (options.computeFunctionSideEffects) {
396 |       passes.add(markPureFunctions);
397 |     } else if (options.markNoSideEffectCalls) {
398 |       // TODO(user) The properties that this pass adds to CALL and NEW
399 |       // AST nodes increase the AST's in-memory size.  Given that we are
400 |       // already running close to our memory limits, we could run into
401 |       // trouble if we end up using the @nosideeffects annotation a lot
402 |       // or compute @nosideeffects annotations by looking at function
403 |       // bodies.  It should be easy to propagate @nosideeffects
404 |       // annotations as part of passes that depend on this property and
405 |       // store the result outside the AST (which would allow garbage
406 |       // collection once the pass is done).
407 |       passes.add(markNoSideEffectCalls);
408 |     }
409 | 
410 |     if (options.chainCalls) {
411 |       passes.add(chainCalls);
412 |     }
413 | 
414 |     // Constant checking must be done after property collapsing because
415 |     // property collapsing can introduce new constants (e.g. enum values).
416 |     if (options.inlineConstantVars) {
417 |       passes.add(checkConsts);
418 |     }
419 | 
420 |     // The Caja library adds properties to Object.prototype, which breaks
421 |     // most for-in loops.  This adds a check to each loop that skips
422 |     // any property matching /___$/.
423 |     if (options.ignoreCajaProperties) {
424 |       passes.add(ignoreCajaProperties);
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     assertAllOneTimePasses(passes);
428 | 
429 |     if (options.smartNameRemoval || options.reportPath != null) {
430 |       passes.addAll(getCodeRemovingPasses());
431 |       passes.add(smartNamePass);
432 |     }
433 | 
434 |     // This needs to come after the inline constants pass, which is run within
435 |     // the code removing passes.
436 |     if (options.closurePass) {
437 |       passes.add(closureOptimizePrimitives);
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // TODO(user): This forces a first crack at crossModuleCodeMotion
441 |     // before devirtualization. Once certain functions are devirtualized,
442 |     // it confuses crossModuleCodeMotion ability to recognized that
443 |     // it is recursive.
444 | 
445 |     // TODO(user): This is meant for a temporary quick win.
446 |     // In the future, we might want to improve our analysis in
447 |     // CrossModuleCodeMotion so we don't need to do this.
448 |     if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
449 |       passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
450 |     }
451 | 
452 |     // Method devirtualization benefits from property disambiguiation so
453 |     // it should run after that pass but before passes that do
454 |     // optimizations based on global names (like cross module code motion
455 |     // and inline functions).  Smart Name Removal does better if run before
456 |     // this pass.
457 |     if (options.devirtualizePrototypeMethods) {
458 |       passes.add(devirtualizePrototypeMethods);
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     if (options.customPasses != null) {
462 |       passes.add(getCustomPasses(
463 |           CustomPassExecutionTime.BEFORE_OPTIMIZATION_LOOP));
464 |     }
465 | 
466 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeMainOptimizations"));
467 | 
468 |     if (options.specializeInitialModule) {
469 |       // When specializing the initial module, we want our fixups to be
470 |       // as lean as possible, so we run the entire optimization loop to a
471 |       // fixed point before specializing, then specialize, and then run the
472 |       // main optimization loop again.
473 | 
474 |       passes.addAll(getMainOptimizationLoop());
475 | 
476 |       if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
477 |         passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
478 |       }
479 | 
480 |       if (options.crossModuleMethodMotion) {
481 |         passes.add(crossModuleMethodMotion);
482 |       }
483 | 
484 |       passes.add(specializeInitialModule.makeOneTimePass());
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     passes.addAll(getMainOptimizationLoop());
488 | 
489 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeModuleMotion"));
490 | 
491 |     if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
492 |       passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
493 |     }
494 | 
495 |     if (options.crossModuleMethodMotion) {
496 |       passes.add(crossModuleMethodMotion);
497 |     }
498 | 
499 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("afterModuleMotion"));
500 | 
501 |     // Some optimizations belong outside the loop because running them more
502 |     // than once would either have no benefit or be incorrect.
503 |     if (options.customPasses != null) {
504 |       passes.add(getCustomPasses(
505 |           CustomPassExecutionTime.AFTER_OPTIMIZATION_LOOP));
506 |     }
507 | 
508 |     if (options.flowSensitiveInlineVariables) {
509 |       passes.add(flowSensitiveInlineVariables);
510 | 
511 |       // After inlining some of the variable uses, some variables are unused.
512 |       // Re-run remove unused vars to clean it up.
513 |       if (options.removeUnusedVars || options.removeUnusedLocalVars) {
514 |         passes.add(removeUnusedVars);
515 |       }
516 |     }
517 | 
518 |     // Running this pass again is required to have goog.events compile down to
519 |     // nothing when compiled on its own.
520 |     if (options.smartNameRemoval) {
521 |       passes.add(smartNamePass2);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     if (options.collapseAnonymousFunctions) {
525 |       passes.add(collapseAnonymousFunctions);
526 |     }
527 | 
528 |     // Move functions before extracting prototype member declarations.
529 |     if (options.moveFunctionDeclarations ||
530 |         // renamePrefixNamescape relies on moveFunctionDeclarations
531 |         // to preserve semantics.
532 |         options.renamePrefixNamespace != null) {
533 |       passes.add(moveFunctionDeclarations);
534 |     }
535 | 
536 |     if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
537 |         AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.MAPPED) {
538 |       passes.add(nameMappedAnonymousFunctions);
539 |     }
540 | 
541 |     // The mapped name anonymous function pass makes use of information that
542 |     // the extract prototype member declarations pass removes so the former
543 |     // happens before the latter.
544 |     //
545 |     // Extracting prototype properties screws up the heuristic renaming
546 |     // policies, so never run it when those policies are requested.
547 |     if (options.extractPrototypeMemberDeclarations &&
548 |         (options.propertyRenaming != PropertyRenamingPolicy.HEURISTIC &&
549 |          options.propertyRenaming !=
550 |             PropertyRenamingPolicy.AGGRESSIVE_HEURISTIC)) {
551 |       passes.add(extractPrototypeMemberDeclarations);
552 |     }
553 | 
554 |     if (options.ambiguateProperties &&
555 |         (options.propertyRenaming == PropertyRenamingPolicy.ALL_UNQUOTED)) {
556 |       passes.add(ambiguateProperties);
557 |     }
558 | 
559 |     if (options.propertyRenaming != PropertyRenamingPolicy.OFF) {
560 |       passes.add(renameProperties);
561 |     }
562 | 
563 |     // Reserve global names added to the "windows" object.
564 |     if (options.reserveRawExports) {
565 |       passes.add(gatherRawExports);
566 |     }
567 | 
568 |     // This comes after property renaming because quoted property names must
569 |     // not be renamed.
570 |     if (options.convertToDottedProperties) {
571 |       passes.add(convertToDottedProperties);
572 |     }
573 | 
574 |     // Property renaming must happen before this pass runs since this
575 |     // pass may convert dotted properties into quoted properties.  It
576 |     // is beneficial to run before alias strings, alias keywords and
577 |     // variable renaming.
578 |     if (options.rewriteFunctionExpressions) {
579 |       passes.add(rewriteFunctionExpressions);
580 |     }
581 | 
582 |     // This comes after converting quoted property accesses to dotted property
583 |     // accesses in order to avoid aliasing property names.
584 |     if (!options.aliasableStrings.isEmpty() || options.aliasAllStrings) {
585 |       passes.add(aliasStrings);
586 |     }
587 | 
588 |     if (options.aliasExternals) {
589 |       passes.add(aliasExternals);
590 |     }
591 | 
592 |     if (options.aliasKeywords) {
593 |       passes.add(aliasKeywords);
594 |     }
595 | 
596 |     // Passes after this point can no longer depend on normalized AST
597 |     // assumptions.
598 |     passes.add(markUnnormalized);
599 | 
600 |     if (options.coalesceVariableNames) {
601 |       passes.add(coalesceVariableNames);
602 | 
603 |       // coalesceVariables creates identity assignments and more redundant code
604 |       // that can be removed, rerun the peephole optimizations to clean them
605 |       // up.
606 |       if (options.foldConstants) {
607 |         passes.add(peepholeOptimizations);
608 |       }
609 |     }
610 | 
611 |     if (options.collapseVariableDeclarations) {
612 |       passes.add(exploitAssign);
613 |       passes.add(collapseVariableDeclarations);
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     // This pass works best after collapseVariableDeclarations.
617 |     passes.add(denormalize);
618 | 
619 |     if (options.instrumentationTemplate != null) {
620 |       passes.add(instrumentFunctions);
621 |     }
622 | 
623 |     if (options.variableRenaming != VariableRenamingPolicy.ALL) {
624 |       // If we're leaving some (or all) variables with their old names,
625 |       // then we need to undo any of the markers we added for distinguishing
626 |       // local variables ("$$1").
627 |       passes.add(invertContextualRenaming);
628 |     }
629 | 
630 |     if (options.variableRenaming != VariableRenamingPolicy.OFF) {
631 |       passes.add(renameVars);
632 |     }
633 | 
634 |     if (options.groupVariableDeclarations) {
635 |       passes.add(groupVariableDeclarations);
636 |     }
637 | 
638 |     // This pass should run after names stop changing.
639 |     if (options.processObjectPropertyString) {
640 |       passes.add(objectPropertyStringPostprocess);
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     if (options.labelRenaming) {
644 |       passes.add(renameLabels);
645 |     }
646 | 
647 |     if (options.foldConstants) {
648 |       passes.add(latePeepholeOptimizations);
649 |     }
650 | 
651 |     if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
652 |         AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.UNMAPPED) {
653 |       passes.add(nameUnmappedAnonymousFunctions);
654 |     }
655 | 
656 |     if (options.renamePrefixNamespace != null) {
657 |       if (!GLOBAL_SYMBOL_NAMESPACE_PATTERN.matcher(
658 |           options.renamePrefixNamespace).matches()) {
659 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException(
660 |             "Illegal character in renamePrefixNamespace name: "
661 |             + options.renamePrefixNamespace);
662 |       }
663 |       passes.add(rescopeGlobalSymbols);
664 |     }
665 | 
666 |     passes.add(stripSideEffectProtection);
667 | 
668 |     // Safety checks
669 |     passes.add(sanityCheckAst);
670 |     passes.add(sanityCheckVars);
671 | 
672 |     return passes;
673 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 123-151
Documentation:

Method code:
123 |   private void overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo> allDefines) {
124 |     boolean changed = false;
125 |     for (Map.Entry<String, DefineInfo> def : allDefines.entrySet()) {
126 |       String defineName = def.getKey();
127 |       DefineInfo info = def.getValue();
128 |       Node inputValue = dominantReplacements.get(defineName);
129 |       Node finalValue = inputValue != null ?
130 |           inputValue : info.getLastValue();
131 |       if (finalValue != info.initialValue) {
132 |         info.initialValueParent.replaceChild(
133 |             info.initialValue, finalValue.cloneTree());
134 |         compiler.addToDebugLog("Overriding @define variable " + defineName);
135 |         changed = changed ||
136 |             finalValue.getType() != info.initialValue.getType() ||
137 |             !finalValue.isEquivalentTo(info.initialValue);
138 |       }
139 |     }
140 | 
141 |     if (changed) {
142 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     Set<String> unusedReplacements = dominantReplacements.keySet();
146 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(allDefines.keySet());
147 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(KNOWN_DEFINES);
148 |     for (String unknownDefine : unusedReplacements) {
149 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(UNKNOWN_DEFINE_WARNING, unknownDefine));
150 |     }
151 |   }

===== M0812 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.StripProtection.StripProtection(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java

===== M0820 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0840 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0843 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Denormalize.maybeCollapseIntoForStatements(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Denormalize.java
Original range: 65-127
Documentation:
/**
   * Collapse VARs and EXPR_RESULT node into FOR loop initializers where
   * possible.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private void maybeCollapseIntoForStatements(Node n, Node parent) {
66 |     // Only SCRIPT, BLOCK, and LABELs can have FORs that can be collapsed into.
67 |     // LABELs are not supported here.
68 |     if (parent == null || !NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent)) {
69 |       return;
70 |     }
71 | 
72 |     // Is the current node something that can be in a for loop initializer?
73 |     if (!n.isExprResult() && !n.isVar()) {
74 |       return;
75 |     }
76 | 
77 |     // Is the next statement a valid FOR?
78 |     Node nextSibling = n.getNext();
79 |     if (nextSibling == null) {
80 |       return;
81 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isForIn(nextSibling)) {
82 |       Node forNode = nextSibling;
83 |       Node forVar = forNode.getFirstChild();
84 |       if (forVar.isName()
85 |           && n.isVar() && n.hasOneChild()) {
86 |         Node name = n.getFirstChild();
87 |         if (!name.hasChildren()
88 |             && forVar.getString().equals(name.getString())) {
89 |           // Ok, the names match, and the var declaration does not have an
90 |           // initializer. Move it into the loop.
91 |           parent.removeChild(n);
92 |           forNode.replaceChild(forVar, n);
93 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
94 |         }
95 |       }
96 |     } else if (nextSibling.isFor()
97 |         && nextSibling.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
98 | 
99 |       // Does the current node contain an in operator?  If so, embedding
100 |       // the expression in a for loop can cause some Javascript parsers (such
101 |       // as the Playstation 3's browser based on Access's NetFront
102 |       // browser) to fail to parse the code.
103 |       // See bug 1778863 for details.
104 |       if (NodeUtil.containsType(n, Token.IN)) {
105 |         return;
106 |       }
107 | 
108 |       // Move the current node into the FOR loop initializer.
109 |       Node forNode = nextSibling;
110 |       Node oldInitializer = forNode.getFirstChild();
111 |       parent.removeChild(n);
112 | 
113 |       Node newInitializer;
114 |       if (n.isVar()) {
115 |         newInitializer = n;
116 |       } else {
117 |         // Extract the expression from EXPR_RESULT node.
118 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
119 |         newInitializer = n.getFirstChild();
120 |         n.removeChild(newInitializer);
121 |       }
122 | 
123 |       forNode.replaceChild(oldInitializer, newInitializer);
124 | 
125 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
126 |     }
127 |   }
